select p.country, year(r.reading_ts) yr, month(r.reading_ts) mo, count(*) joined_rows, count(p.installed_kwp) non_null_capacity, sum(r.energy_kwh) energy, avg(r.irradiance_wm2) irradiance, sum(r.energy_kwh) / nullif(sum(p.installed_kwp),0) specific_yield from fact_solar_readings r inner join (select * from dim_plant where country in ('ES','IT')) p on r.plant_id=p.plant_id where r.reading_ts >= '2020-01-01' and r.reading_ts < '2025-01-01' group by p.country, year(r.reading_ts), month(r.reading_ts) order by p.country, yr, mo

create table dim_plant as (select cast((x-1)/1 as int) plant_id, cast(case when (x-1)/1 < 10000 then case when ((x-1)/1)%2=0 then 'ES' else 'IT' end else 'DE' end as symbol) country, rnd_int(1,800,0)/8.0 installed_kwp, timestamp_sequence('2020-01-01',1578528000) reading_ts from long_sequence(100000,130,131)) timestamp(reading_ts) partition by month bypass wal;
